...RESPONDENTS
Petition under Adicle 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac-2400.00 gts., in Sy-No,7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee Wo. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of smt. Haneefa Bee viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representalion dt. 31-o3-2o23 followed

24
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 2'l and 30O'A of the Conslittuion of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
Memorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under section 15'l cPC praying that in the circumstances slated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate sleps pursuant to the
representation dt_ 31-03-2023, pending disposal of the main writ Petition, in the
interest of justice.

lol
Petition under Article 226 of the Conslitrrttion of lndia praying that in the
circumslances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased [o issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasu!.ing more than Ac.
2.400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandel,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
hrlemorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 2O-O3-2O23 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300-4 of the Constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peacelul and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
Memorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dl.20-03-2023, pending disposal of lhe main Wrat petition, in the
interest of justice.

Petition under Arlicle 226 of the constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate writ, order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400- 00 gts., in Sy. No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee w/o. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allntted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md- Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt- Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dl. 21- 10- 2023 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjusl u nconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300- A of the constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peacefulandphysicalpossessionoftheplotstothepet.tionersaSpertheir
lrilemorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under section 1 51 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant lo the
representation dt. 21- 10- 2023, pending disposal of the main writ Petition, in the
interest of justice.

...RESPONDENTS
Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land adrieasuring more than Ac.
2400-00 gts., in Sy-No.7, siluated at lvlansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District. which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee Wo. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the wrilten representation dl. 2A.06-2023 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutioneil and violative
of Articles 21 and 300-A of the Constitution of lndia and consequenty direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
Memorandum of Gift, and to grant such other relief or reliefs as this lionibie Court
deems fit and proper in the circumstances of the case and in the interest of justice.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumslances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dt.28-06-2023, pending disposal of the main Wril Petition, in the
interest of justice.

...RESPONDENTS

LL-L-Petition under Anicle 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of ltrtandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in laking steps to release the entire iand admeasuring more than Ac-2400.00 gts., in Sy.No-7, situated at lVlansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee Wo. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made inlo small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt' Haneefa Bee
viz. , Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
lvlemorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt.21-1o-2O23 followed
by personat visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300-A of the constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and lo deliver the vacant,
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
lvlemorandum of Gift, and to grant such other relief or reliefs as this Honourable
court deems fit and proper in the circumstances of the case and in the interest of
iustice.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under section 151 CPC praying that in lhe circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dt.21-1O-2O23, pending disposal of the main writ Petition, in the
interest of justice.

...RESPONDENTS
Petition under Nlicle 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased [o issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direclion more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of lhe respondent
aulhorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac_
2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy Dislrict, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care laker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent .of 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., tvld. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 15-03-2023 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Artrcles 21 and 300-A of the Constitution of lndia and consequen y direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
Memorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affrdavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities lo consider and take appropriate sleps pursuant to the
representation dt.15-O4-2O23, pending disposal of the main Writ petition, in the
interest of justice.

Petition under Article 226 of lhe constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate writ, order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the enlire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400.00 gts., in sy.No.7, situated at lvlansoorabad village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee wo. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 13'04-2023 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300-4 of the constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
Memorandum of Gift-lA NO: 1 OF 2024
petition under Section 151 cPc praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the Higth court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation da.13-O1-2O23, pending disposal of the main writ Petition, in the
interest of iustice.

...RESPONDENTS
Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400.00 gts-, in Sy. No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o. Burhanuddin. and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Srnt. Haneefa Bee
viz-, tvld. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 21-O3-2O23 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300-4 of the constitution of lndia and consequen y direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,

93'
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
Memorandum of Gift.
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dt.21-O3-2O23, pending disposal of the main Writ Petition, in the
interest of justice.

3L)-
Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particulady one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400- 00 gts., in Sy. No. 7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 1 00 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 09- 10- 2023 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, uniust unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300- A of the Constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant.
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
Memorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit faled in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dt. 09- 10- 2023, pending disposal of the main Writ Petition, in the
interest of justice.

Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac-2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., I\r1d. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Nrlemorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt.31-O3-2O23 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300-A of the Constitution of lndia, and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant.
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
ltlemorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dt. 3110312023, pending disposal of the main Writ Petition, in the
interest of justice.

qfi
Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of India praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particulady one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsami-na by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 27 -O3-2O23 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300-4 of the Constittuion of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peaceful and physica{ possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
Itilemorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dt.27-O3-2O23, pending disposal of the main Writ Petition, in the
interest of justice.

...RESPONDENTS
Petition under Article 226 ot lhe Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of lvlandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac-2400.00 gts., in Sy.No-7, situated at ft/lansoorabad Village, Saroornagar lvlandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 28-04-2023 followed
by personal visits, as being illegat, arbilrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300-A of the Constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
Memorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 1 51 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dt.28-04-2023, pending disposal of the main Writ Petition, in the
interest of iustice.

...RESPONDENTS
Petition under Article 226 ot the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400.00 gts., in Sy-No.7, situated at lvlansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee Wo. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., tvld - Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
lvlemorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 2043-2023 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300-4 of the Constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
Memorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit fited in support of the petition, the High court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dt.2o.O3 -2023, pending disposal of the main Writ Petition, in the
interest of justice.

...RESPONDENTS
Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac-24OO.O0 gts., in Sy.No-7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee Wo. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz,, Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 15-O4-2O23 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300-A of the Constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authoritres to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
Memorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consader and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dtj5-O4-2l23, pending disposal of the main Writ Petition, in the
interest of iustice.

Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400.00 gts-, in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar lvlandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 1 00 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz.. Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 2710312023 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 30O-A of the Constitution of India and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to !'elease the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
ft/emorandum of Gift, and to grant such other relief or reliefs as this Honourable

{9e
Court deems fit and proper in the circumstances of the case and in the interest of
iustice.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High Court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dt. 2710312023, pending disposal or the main Wrat Petition, in the
interest of justice.

...RESPONDENTS
Petition under Article 226 of lhe Constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particulady one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac.
2400-00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
lv,lemorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 2710312023 followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, un.iust unconstitutional and vio{ative of
Articles 21 and 300-4 of the constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
lvlemorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 1 OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dt.27-03-2023, pending disposal of the main writ Petition, in the
interest of justice.

2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Nilansoorabad village, saroornagar lvlandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the iand made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq' Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt- Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Nlemorandum of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 27 -O3-2O23, followed
by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300-4 of the Constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
Memorandum of Gift.

Petition under section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dt.27-03-2023, pending disposal of the main writ Petition, in the
irrterest of justice.

Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of India praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of lvlandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring more than Ac
2400.00 gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made tnto small plots to an extent of 1O0 Sq- Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt- Haneefa Bee
viz., Ivld. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasam Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum'of Gift, inspite of the written representation dt. 27 -O3-2O23 followed
by personal visits. as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and violative of
Articles 21 and 300-A of the constitution of lndia and consequently direct the
respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the vacant,
peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
Memorandum of Gift.
lA NO: 'f OF 2024
Petition under Section 151 CPC praying that in the circumstances stated in
the affidavit filed in support of the petition, the High court may be pleased to direct
the respondent authorities to consider and take appropriate steps pursuant to the
representation dt.27-03-2023, pending disposal of the main writ Petition, in the
interest of ,ustice.

Petition under Article 226 of the Constitution of India praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High Court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate Writ, Order or Direction more particularly one in
the nature of Writ of Mandamus, declaring the inaction of the respondent
authorities in taking steps to release the entire land admeasuring moi.e than Ac.
24OO.OO gts., in Sy.No.7, situated at Mansoorabad Village, Saroornagar Mandal,
Ranga Reddy District, which was kept under the Custody of the Government as
care taker by the original pattedar Smt. Haneefa Bee W/o. Burhanuddin, and in
view of the land made into small plots to an extent of 100 Sq. Yards to each
petitioner and allotted to the petitioners by the legal heirs of Smt. Haneefa Bee
viz., Md. Yousuf Khan and his wife Smt. Vasarn Tulsamma by way of
Memorandum of Gift, inspite of the written Rep.resefltation dl. 2310312023,

e9x
followed by personal visits, as being illegal, arbitrary, unjust unconstitutional and
violative of Articles 21 and 300-4 of the Constitution of lndia and consequently
direct the respondent authorities to release the entire land and to deliver the
vacant, peaceful and physical possession of the plots to the petitioners as per their
Memorandum of Gift.

17 - In the opinion of this Court., the petitioners lack foundational
claim. Even accolding to the petitioners their predecessors were
not in possession ofthe subject land for the last seventy (70) years
and that since 1947, the subject land had been in posscssion of the
